---

## għejja (v-għejja)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: għ-j-j
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to exhaust, to weary
    **MT**: Ħadem lil xi ħadd stajn u bla enerġija (exhaust / weary).
- **Alternative Forms**: għajja
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Il-vjaġġ twil għejjah u raqad hekk kif wasal.
    EN: The long journey exhausted him and he slept as soon as he arrived.

---

## għejnilla (n-għejnilla)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: diminutive of għonnella
    **MT**: Forma dimminutiva ta' għonnella, libsa tradizzjonali żgħira Maltija (small traditional Maltese cloak).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: L-għejnilla kienet tinġarr kull nhar ta' messa fl-aħħar seklu.
    EN: The small cloak was carried every Sunday Mass in the last century.

---

## għejra (n-għejra)
- **POS**: noun
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: jealousy, envy
    **MT**: Sens ta' inviżja u mibgħeda fuq il-kisbiet ta' ħaddieħor (jealousy / envy).
- **Alternative Forms**: għira
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: L-għejra tiegħu fuq is-suċċess tal-ħabib kienet evidenti.
    EN: His jealousy of his friend's success was evident.

---

## għejxien (n-għejxien)
- **POS**: noun
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: livelihood
    **MT**: Il-mezzi u l-mod ta' ħajja, sostenn (livelihood / living / survival).
  - **EN**: one's living
    **MT**: Il-mezzi u l-mod ta' ħajja, sostenn (livelihood / living / survival).
  - **EN**: survival
    **MT**: Il-mezzi u l-mod ta' ħajja, sostenn (livelihood / living / survival).
  - **EN**: living
    **MT**: Il-mezzi u l-mod ta' ħajja, sostenn (livelihood / living / survival).
- **Alternative Forms**: għajxien
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Sibew l-għejxien tagħhom fl-industrija tas-sajd.
    EN: They found their livelihood in the fishing industry.

---

## għela (v-għela)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: għ-l-j
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to boil
    **MT**: Laħaqt it-temperatura ta' gass (boil).
  - **EN**: to be boiled
    **MT**: Żied il-prezz u dar iktar għali (rise in price).
  - **EN**: to be bitter
    **MT**: Laħaqt it-temperatura ta' gass (boil).
  - **EN**: to feel bitterness
    **MT**: Laħaqt it-temperatura ta' gass (boil).
  - **EN**: to rise in price
    **MT**: Laħaqt it-temperatura ta' gass (boil).
  - **EN**: to become expensive
    **MT**: Laħaqt it-temperatura ta' gass (boil).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: L-ilma għela u l-pasta tqegħdet ġo fih.
    EN: The water boiled and the pasta was placed in it.

---

## għeleb (v-għeleb)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: għ-l-b
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to overcome, to defeat, to win out over
    **MT**: Rebħt u qbilt fuq xi ħadd jew xi ħaġa (overcome / defeat / win out over).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Għeleb l-avversarju bl-intelligenza u l-qawwa tiegħu.
    EN: He overcame the opponent with his intelligence and strength.

---

## għelet (v-għelet)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: għ-l-t
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to make a mistake, to err
    **MT**: Żbaljajt u waqajt f'żball (make a mistake / err).
- **Alternative Forms**: għalat
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Għelet fit-tweġiba u kellu jagħmilha mill-ġdid.
    EN: He erred in the answer and had to redo it.

---

## għelib (n-għelib)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Root**: għ-l-b
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: verbal noun of għeleb
    **MT**: In-nom verbal ta' għeleb, il-proċess u r-riżultat tar-rebħ.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: L-għelib fuq il-mard kien ġej minn determinazzjoni qawwija.
    EN: The overcoming of the illness came from strong determination.

---

## għeliem (n-għeliem)
- **POS**: noun
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: plural of għelm
    **MT**: Plural ta' għelm, serventi jew skjavi.
  - **EN**: servant [obsolete]
    **MT**: Plural ta' għelm, serventi jew skjavi.
  - **EN**: slave [obsolete]
    **MT**: Plural ta' għelm, serventi jew skjavi.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: L-għeliem tal-qorti kienu dejjem jgħożżu d-direttivi tal-mexxej.
    EN: The court servants always respected the leader's directives.

---

## għelk (n-għelk)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Root**: għ-l-k
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: gluey substance
    **MT**: Sustanza twaħħal u viskuża, għalk (gluey substance).
- **Alternative Forms**: għalk
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: L-għelk fuq il-pjanta kkattiva l-insetti żgħar.
    EN: The gluey substance on the plant trapped the small insects.

---

## għella (n-għella)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Root**: għ-l-l
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: misfortune, bad luck
    **MT**: Disfatta u avversità kbira (misfortune / bad luck).
  - **EN**: illness, disease
    **MT**: Marda jew disturb tas-saħħa (illness / disease).
- **Alternative Forms**: għilla
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: L-għella waħdha ma taqtax il-bniedem minn daqqa waħda.
    EN: Misfortune alone does not bring a person down all at once.

---

## għelm (n-għelm)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Root**: għ-l-m
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: sign, mark
    **MT**: Sinjal jew markatrur ta' identifikazzjoni (sign / mark / label / tag).
  - **EN**: tag, label
    **MT**: Sinjal jew markatrur ta' identifikazzjoni (sign / mark / label / tag).
  - **EN**: knowledge [archaic]
    **MT**: Sinjal jew markatrur ta' identifikazzjoni (sign / mark / label / tag).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Qiegħed l-għelm fuq il-pakkett biex jissaħħaħ min ta' hu.
    EN: He placed the mark on the package to confirm whose it was.

---

## għelt (n-għelt)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Root**: għ-l-t
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: mistake, error
    **MT**: Żball jew ħata f'xi ħaġa (mistake / error).
- **Alternative Forms**: għalt
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Sab l-għelt fl-espressjoni u rranga l-kitba.
    EN: He found the error in the expression and corrected the writing.

---

## għeluq (n-għeluq)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Root**: għ-l-q
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: verbal noun of għalaq: closing, closure
    **MT**: In-nom verbal ta' għalaq, l-att ta' għeluq (closing / closure).
  - **EN**: ending, end
    **MT**: It-tmiem ta' xi ħaġa (ending / end).
- **Alternative Forms**: egħluq
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: L-għeluq tal-festival kien immarkat b'spettaklu kbir.
    EN: The closing of the festival was marked by a grand spectacle.

---

## għeluq snin (n-għeluq-snin)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: birthday
    **MT**: Il-jum anniversarju tat-twelid ta' xi ħadd (birthday).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Iċċelebrajna l-għeluq snin tagħha f'restawrant sabiħ.
    EN: We celebrated her birthday in a beautiful restaurant.

---

## għeluqija (n-għeluqija)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Root**: għ-l-q
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: narrowness
    **MT**: Il-kwalità ta' xi ħaġa dejqa u mingħajr spazju (narrowness).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: L-għeluqija tal-toroq f'dawn l-inħawi kienet problem kbira.
    EN: The narrowness of the roads in these parts was a great problem.

---

## għemeż (v-għemeż)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: għ-m-ż
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to wink, blink (at)
    **MT**: Għalaqt u ftaqt minn rasi waħda b'mod mgħaġġel (wink / blink).
- **Alternative Forms**: għameż
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Għemeż lejhom biex juri li fehem l-intenzjoni.
    EN: He winked at them to show he understood the intention.

---

## għemiż (n-għemiż)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Root**: għ-m-ż
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: verbal noun of għameż
    **MT**: In-nom verbal ta' għameż u għemeż, l-att ta' tagħmix.
  - **EN**: verbal noun of għemeż
    **MT**: In-nom verbal ta' għameż u għemeż, l-att ta' tagħmix.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: L-għemiż tiegħu fis-suq kien sinjal ta' qbil.
    EN: His wink in the market was a sign of agreement.

---

## għen (v-għen)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: għ-j-n
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to help
    **MT**: Tajt assistenza u appoġġ lil xi ħadd fi ħtieġa (help).
- **Alternative Forms**: għan
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Għen lill-anzjan iġorr il-bkiek sal-bieb.
    EN: He helped the elderly man carry the bags to the door.

---

## għeneb (n-għeneb)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: grape(s)
    **MT**: Frotta żgħira u ħelwa li tikber f'gruppi fuq id-dielja (grape).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: L-għeneb tal-Malti huwa magħruf għat-togħma ħelwa tiegħu.
    EN: Maltese grapes are known for their sweet taste.

---

## għenejqed (n-għenejqed)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Root**: għ-n-q-d
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: diminutive of għanqud
    **MT**: Forma dimminutiva ta' għanqud, feqgħa żgħira tal-frott (small bunch of fruit).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Ħa ftit għenejqed tal-għeneb mis-siġra.
    EN: He took a small bunch of grapes from the vine.

---

## għenelli (adv-għenelli)
- **POS**: adverb
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: it seems to me
    **MT**: Jidher jew jidhirli, għawnelli (it seems to me).
- **Alternative Forms**: għawnelli
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Għenelli li l-avveniment se jirnexxi.
    EN: It seems to me that the event will succeed.

---

## għens (n-għens)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Root**: għ-n-s
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: billygoat
    **MT**: Mogħoż raġel tal-qagħda tal-familja (billygoat).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: L-għens kien isaltan fil-gnien kull filgħodu.
    EN: The billygoat roamed the garden every morning.

---

## għepp (n-għepp)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: yelp (a short, high-pitched cry or bark, especially made by a young puppy before it learns to bark properly)
    **MT**: Għajta qasira u qawwija ta' kelb żgħir (yelp).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Sema' l-għepp ta' ġrieden ċkejknin mit-tinda.
    EN: He heard the yelp of little puppies from the tent.

---

## għepp (v-għepp)
- **POS**: verb
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to yip (used of puppies)
    **MT**: Ħarreġ jgħajjat b'mod qasir u mgħaġġel (yip - of puppies).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Il-ġrieden bdew jgħepp hekk kif ġie l-ikel.
    EN: The puppies began to yip as soon as the food arrived.

---

## għer (v-għer)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: għ-j-r
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to be jealous of
    **MT**: Ħassejt inviżja fuq xi ħadd (be jealous of / envy).
  - **EN**: to envy
    **MT**: Ħassejt inviżja fuq xi ħadd (be jealous of / envy).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Beda jgħer fuq is-suċċess ta' ħuh fil-karriera.
    EN: He began to envy his brother's career success.

---

## għera (n-għera)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Root**: għ-r-j
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: nakedness, nudity
    **MT**: Il-qagħda ta' jekk tkun bla ħwejjeġ (nakedness / nudity).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: L-għera kienet komuni fl-arti Grieg kklassiku.
    EN: Nudity was common in classical Greek art.

---

## għereq (v-għereq)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: għ-r-q
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to sweat
    **MT**: Ħarġu l-irżiħ mill-ġisem minħabba sħana (sweat / exude).
  - **EN**: to exude
    **MT**: Neżlejt fl-ilma jew ingħereqt (drown / sink / submerge).
  - **EN**: to drown
    **MT**: Ħarġu l-irżiħ mill-ġisem minħabba sħana (sweat / exude).
  - **EN**: to sink, founder
    **MT**: Ħarġu l-irżiħ mill-ġisem minħabba sħana (sweat / exude).
  - **EN**: to submerge
    **MT**: Ħarġu l-irżiħ mill-ġisem minħabba sħana (sweat / exude).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Għereq waqt it-taħriġ tas-siegħa sħiħa.
    EN: He sweated during the full hour of training.

---

## għerf (n-għerf)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Root**: għ-r-f
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: knowledge, wisdom
    **MT**: L-intelliġenza u s-sapjenza li tiġi mill-esperjenza u l-istudju (knowledge / wisdom).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: L-għerf tal-anzjani huwa riżors li m'għandux jinħela.
    EN: The wisdom of the elderly is a resource that should not be wasted.

---

## għeri (adj-għeri)
- **POS**: adjective
- **Root**: għ-r-j
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: naked
    **MT**: Bla ħwejjeġ, naked.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Il-bniedem antik kien jgħix u jaħdem fl-ambjent naturali tiegħu.
    EN: The ancient man lived and worked in his natural environment.

---

## għerik (n-għerik)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Root**: għ-r-k
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: verbal noun of għorok
    **MT**: In-nom verbal ta' għorok, l-att ta' ħakk u attrittura.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: L-għerik tal-karta fuq il-qroll ħalliha ħafna trab.
    EN: The rubbing of the paper on the sandpaper left a lot of dust.

---

## għerq (n-għerq)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Root**: għ-r-q
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: root
    **MT**: Il-parti tal-pjanta li tkun taħt il-ħamrija u tieħu n-nutrijenti (root / origin / beginning).
  - **EN**: nerve
    **MT**: Il-parti tal-pjanta li tkun taħt il-ħamrija u tieħu n-nutrijenti (root / origin / beginning).
  - **EN**: beginning, commencement, origin
    **MT**: Il-parti tal-pjanta li tkun taħt il-ħamrija u tieħu n-nutrijenti (root / origin / beginning).
  - **EN**: root
    **MT**: Il-parti tal-pjanta li tkun taħt il-ħamrija u tieħu n-nutrijenti (root / origin / beginning).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: L-għeruq tal-siġra kienu qodma u kienu jgħaddu taħt ir-raħal.
    EN: The roots of the tree were ancient and ran under the village.

---

## għerusija (n-għerusija)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Root**: għ-r-s
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: betrothal
    **MT**: Il-perjodu u l-istat tal-fidanzament uffiċjali qabel iż-żwieġ (betrothal).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: L-għerusija tagħhom damet sena qabel iż-żwieġ.
    EN: Their betrothal lasted a year before the wedding.

---

## Għesaw (n-għesaw)
- **POS**: noun
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: Esau (male personal name)
    **MT**: Isem personali maskili mill-Bibbja, ħu Ġakbu (Esau).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: L-Għesaw kien il-qaddis ta' Ġakbu fl-Iskrittura.
    EN: Esau was the brother of Jacob in the Scriptures.

---

## għex (v-għex)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: għ-j-x
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to live (be alive)
    **MT**: Kont ħaj u b'saħħtek (live / be alive).
  - **EN**: to live, dwell (have permanent residence)
    **MT**: Ħajt u rqajt f'post speċifiku (live / dwell).
  - **EN**: to live something, to experience
    **MT**: Esperjenzajt xi ħaġa b'mod profond (live something / experience).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Għex ħajja ta' ħidma u dedikazzjonii fis-servizz pubbliku.
    EN: He lived a life of work and dedication in public service.

---

## għeġubija (n-għeġubija)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Root**: għ-ġ-b
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: the quality of being amazing, astonishing, surprising
    **MT**: Il-kwalità ta' xi ħaġa straordinarja (quality of being amazing).
  - **EN**: something amazing, astonishing, surprising
    **MT**: Xi ħaġa mill-isbaħ u straordinarja (a marvel / wonder).
  - **EN**: a marvel, wonder
    **MT**: Il-kwalità ta' xi ħaġa straordinarja (quality of being amazing).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: L-għeġubija tal-arkitettura Maltija tolqotk f'kull rokna.
    EN: The marvel of Maltese architecture strikes you at every corner.

---

## għeżejżen (adj-għeżejżen)
- **POS**: adjective
- **Root**: għ-ż-ż
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: diminutive of għażżien: somewhat lazy
    **MT**: Forma dimminutiva ta' għażżien, ftit pigru (somewhat lazy).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Kien tifel għeżejżen iżda dejjem temm ix-xogħol.
    EN: He was a somewhat lazy boy but always finished the work.

---

## għeżubija (n-għeżubija)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Root**: għ-ż-b
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: bachelorhood
    **MT**: Il-qagħda u l-istat ta' żgħożija bla żwieġ (bachelorhood).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: L-għeżubija kienet bħal libertà li ħadd ma jixtieq jitlef.
    EN: Bachelorhood was like a freedom no one wanted to lose.

---

## għid (n-għid)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Root**: għ-j-d
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: feast, festival,
    **MT**: Ċelebrazzjon jew festa reljiġjuża jew pubblika importanti (feast / festival / holiday / celebration).
  - **EN**: holiday
    **MT**: Ċelebrazzjon jew festa reljiġjuża jew pubblika importanti (feast / festival / holiday / celebration).
  - **EN**: celebration
    **MT**: Ċelebrazzjon jew festa reljiġjuża jew pubblika importanti (feast / festival / holiday / celebration).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: L-Għid il-Kbir huwa l-akbar festa reliġjuża ta' Malta.
    EN: Easter is the greatest religious feast in Malta.

---

## Għid il-Ħamiem (n-għid-il-ħamiem)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: Epiphany
    **MT**: Il-festa tal-Epifanija fl-6 ta' Jannar li tfakkar il-wasla tal-Maġi (Epiphany).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: L-Għid il-Ħamiem jiġi wara l-Milied b'ġimgħatejn.
    EN: Epiphany comes two weeks after Christmas.

---

## Għid il-Ħamsin (n-għid-il-ħamsin)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: Whitsunday, Pentecost
    **MT**: Il-festa tal-Pentekoste ħamsin jum wara l-Qawmien (Whitsunday / Pentecost).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: L-Għid il-Ħamsin jfakkar il-miġja tal-Ispirtu s-Santu.
    EN: Pentecost commemorates the coming of the Holy Spirit.

---

## għilla (n-għilla)
- **POS**: noun
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: misfortune, bad luck
    **MT**: Malafurtuna u marda, għella (misfortune / illness).
  - **EN**: illness, disease
    **MT**: Malafurtuna u marda, għella (misfortune / illness).
- **Alternative Forms**: għella
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: L-għilla waħdha ma twaqqafx lil min ikollu determinazzjonii.
    EN: Misfortune alone does not stop one who has determination.

---

## għira (n-għira)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Root**: għ-j-r
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: jealousy, envy
    **MT**: Inviżja u mibgħeda fuq kisbiet ta' ħaddieħor (jealousy / envy).
- **Alternative Forms**: għejra
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: L-għira kienet id-dgħufija tagħha li ħarbet ħafna ħbiberija.
    EN: Jealousy was her weakness that spoiled many friendships.

---

## Għisa (n-għisa)
- **POS**: noun
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: Jesus [obsolete]
    **MT**: Isem personali Malti ta' Ġesù Kristu jew Joswa (Jesus / Joshua).
  - **EN**: Joshua [obsolete]
    **MT**: Isem personali Malti ta' Ġesù Kristu jew Joswa (Jesus / Joshua).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Għisa Kristu huwa l-ċentru tal-fidi Nisranija.
    EN: Jesus Christ is the centre of the Christian faith.

---

## għixien (n-għixien)
- **POS**: noun
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: livelihood
    **MT**: Il-mod u l-mezzi ta' ħajja, sostenn (livelihood / living / survival).
  - **EN**: one's living
    **MT**: Il-mod u l-mezzi ta' ħajja, sostenn (livelihood / living / survival).
  - **EN**: survival
    **MT**: Il-mod u l-mezzi ta' ħajja, sostenn (livelihood / living / survival).
  - **EN**: living
    **MT**: Il-mod u l-mezzi ta' ħajja, sostenn (livelihood / living / survival).
- **Alternative Forms**: għajxien
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: L-għixien tal-familja kien ibbażat fuq il-qligħ tal-missier.
    EN: The family's livelihood was based on the father's earnings.

---

## għodd (v-għodd)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: għ-d-d
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: imperative singular of għadd
    **MT**: L-imperattiv ta' għadd, agħdodd (count / calculate / matter).
  - **EN**: (transitive) to count, to calculate
    **MT**: L-imperattiv ta' għadd, agħdodd (count / calculate / matter).
  - **EN**: (intransitive) to matter, to be important or valid
    **MT**: L-imperattiv ta' għadd, agħdodd (count / calculate / matter).
  - **EN**: (transitive, + bħal or b’) to consider to be, to take for
    **MT**: L-imperattiv ta' għadd, agħdodd (count / calculate / matter).
- **Alternative Forms**: għadd
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Għodd il-flus u ara kemm fadal.
    EN: Count the money and see how much is left.

---

## għodda (n-għodda)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Root**: għ-d-d
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: tool
    **MT**: Strument jew apparat li jintuża biex jiġi ffaċilitat ix-xogħol (tool / instrument).
  - **EN**: instrument
    **MT**: Strument jew apparat li jintuża biex jiġi ffaċilitat ix-xogħol (tool / instrument).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: L-għodda tal-faċċar kienet antika iżda funzjonali.
    EN: The carpenter's tool was ancient but functional.

## għodos (v-għodos)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: għ-d-s
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to dive
    **MT**: Ġejt taħt l-ilma biex tesplora jew tuza l-imħarek (dive).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Għodos fil-baħar fond biex ikejjel l-ankra.
    EN: He dived in the deep sea to check the anchor.

## għodu (n-għodu)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Root**: għ-d-w
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: morning
    **MT**: Il-parti tal-jum minn wara l-albasija sa nofsinhar (morning).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: L-għodu huwa l-parti favorita tal-jum tiegħu.
    EN: The morning is his favourite part of the day.

---

## għodu għodu (adv-għodu-għodu)
- **POS**: adverb
- **Root**: għ-d-w
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: early morning
    **MT**: Fl-ewwel sigħat tal-ġurnata qabel ma tisħon (early morning).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Jqum għodu għodu biex jaħdem fil-kwiet.
    EN: He wakes up in the early morning to work in silence.

## għodwa (n-għodwa)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Root**: għ-d-w
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: morning
    **MT**: Il-parti tal-jum mill-albasija sa nofsinhar (morning).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Qam kmieni fl-għodwa u marr jixtri l-ħobż ħarir.
    EN: He woke up early in the morning and went to buy fresh bread.

## għola (v-għola)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: għ-l-j
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to ascend
    **MT**: Tlajt 'il fuq minn post baxx (ascend / rise / soar / climb up).
  - **EN**: to rise
    **MT**: Żied il-prezz u dar iktar għali (rise in price / become expensive).
  - **EN**: to soar
    **MT**: Tlajt 'il fuq minn post baxx (ascend / rise / soar / climb up).
  - **EN**: to climb up (move to a higher position)
    **MT**: Tlajt 'il fuq minn post baxx (ascend / rise / soar / climb up).
  - **EN**: to rise
    **MT**: Tlajt 'il fuq minn post baxx (ascend / rise / soar / climb up).
  - **EN**: to increase
    **MT**: Tlajt 'il fuq minn post baxx (ascend / rise / soar / climb up).
  - **EN**: to rise in price
    **MT**: Tlajt 'il fuq minn post baxx (ascend / rise / soar / climb up).
  - **EN**: to become expensive
    **MT**: Tlajt 'il fuq minn post baxx (ascend / rise / soar / climb up).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Għola fuq is-siġra biex jara l-farka tal-qamħ.
    EN: He climbed up the tree to see the wheat field.

## għoli (adj-għoli)
- **POS**: adjective
- **Root**: għ-l-j
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: high
    **MT**: Li jkun 'il fuq fil-livell jew fl-altitudni (high / tall).
  - **EN**: tall
    **MT**: Li jkun b'vuċi b'saħħitha u qawwija (loud).
  - **EN**: loud
    **MT**: Li jkun 'il fuq fil-livell jew fl-altitudni (high / tall).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Il-mħatra kienet għolja wisq biex titla' waħedha.
    EN: The wall was too tall for her to climb alone.

## għolja (adj-għolja)
- **POS**: adjective
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: feminine singular of għoli
    **MT**: Il-forma femminili ta' għoli, li tkun 'il fuq (high / tall - feminine).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Il-bajda għolja kienet taqta' r-riħ minn naħa għal oħra.
    EN: The high bay window cut the wind from side to side.

## għolja (n-għolja)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: hill
    **MT**: Elevazzjoni naturali tal-art li tkun itqal mill-madwar (hill).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: L-għolja kienet tidher minn ħafna bogħod fil-ħalq tal-wied.
    EN: The hill was visible from far away at the mouth of the valley.

## għolla (v-għolla)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: għ-l-j
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to raise
    **MT**: Ġbidt 'il fuq xi ħaġa jew xi ħadd (raise / lift).
  - **EN**: to lift (move something to a higher position)
    **MT**: Żidejt il-prezz ta' xi ħaġa (raise the price).
  - **EN**: to raise (an amount, rate, tax, etc.)
    **MT**: Ġbidt 'il fuq xi ħaġa jew xi ħadd (raise / lift).
  - **EN**: to raise the price of
    **MT**: Ġbidt 'il fuq xi ħaġa jew xi ħadd (raise / lift).
  - **EN**: to make something more expensive
    **MT**: Ġbidt 'il fuq xi ħaġa jew xi ħadd (raise / lift).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Għolla idejh fis-sema bħala gest ta' rebħ.
    EN: He raised his hands to the sky as a gesture of victory.

## għomor (n-għomor)

- **Root**: għ-m-r
- **Definitions**:

  - **EN**: with regard to living persons also: that which has passed of it or that which remains)
    **MT**: It-tul tal-ħajja ta' bniedem jew annimal (lifespan / lifetime / age).
  - **EN**: age (quality of having lived for a particular time)

    **MT**: It-tul tal-ħajja ta' bniedem jew annimal (lifespan / lifetime / age).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: L-għomor ta' din is-siġra kien iktar minn mitt sena.
    EN: The lifespan of this tree was more than a hundred years.

## għonnella (n-għonnella)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: kind of head covering worn by Maltese women (generally until the 19th c. and occasionally still in the earlier 20th c.), most typically in the form of a long shawl with a stiff hem, creating a wide hood over the wearer’s head
    **MT**: Libsa tradizzjonali Maltija b'kappa sewda użata mill-mara biex tgħatti rasha (traditional Maltese women's head covering).
  - **EN**: women’s overgarment more generally [obsolete]
    **MT**: Libsa tradizzjonali Maltija b'kappa sewda użata mill-mara biex tgħatti rasha (traditional Maltese women's head covering).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: L-għonnella kienet tiġi fil-knisja u avvenimenti formali.
    EN: The traditional cloak was worn at church and formal events.

  - **EN**: collar

## għotob (v-għotob)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: għ-t-b
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to become crippled

  - **EN**: to set (a sun, a moon, etc…)
    **MT**: Nazzal u spiċċa taħt il-linja tal-orizzont (set - sun or moon).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Il-ksar waqt il-logħba għotbu u ma qatt ma erġa' jiġri.
    EN: The break during the game crippled him and he never ran again.

- **Alternative Forms**: għatar
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Għotor fuq il-ħaġra u aqba' fl-art.

## għoxa (v-għoxa)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: għ-x-j
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to faint
    **MT**: Tliftu l-kuxjenza (faint).
  - **EN**: to break out in laughter or (less often) tears
    **MT**: Ftaqartli b'daħka jew biki (break out in laughter or tears).
  - **EN**: to enjoy, relish
    **MT**: Stennejt pjaċir kbir minn xi ħaġa (enjoy / relish / be thrilled).
  - **EN**: to indulge in
    **MT**: Tliftu l-kuxjenza (faint).
  - **EN**: to be thrilled by
    **MT**: Tliftu l-kuxjenza (faint).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Għoxet bid-daħka meta sema' l-battuta.
    EN: She broke out in laughter when she heard the joke.

## għuda (n-għuda)

## għul (n-għul)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine

## ġab (v-ġab)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: ġ-j-b
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to bring, to carry
    **MT**: Wassal xi ħaġa jew xi ħadd lejn post ieħor (bring / carry / fetch).
  - **EN**: to procure, to cause
    **MT**: Ikkawża xi ħaġa (procure / cause).
  - **EN**: to fetch (a price), to sell for
    **MT**: Immagina jew stima (imagine / esteem).
  - **EN**: to form, to give shape (to something)
    **MT**: Wassal xi ħaġa jew xi ħadd lejn post ieħor (bring / carry / fetch).
  - **EN**: to work out, to form, to calculate
    **MT**: Wassal xi ħaġa jew xi ħadd lejn post ieħor (bring / carry / fetch).
  - **EN**: to guess, to succeed
    **MT**: Wassal xi ħaġa jew xi ħadd lejn post ieħor (bring / carry / fetch).
  - **EN**: to turn, to change (something)
    **MT**: Wassal xi ħaġa jew xi ħadd lejn post ieħor (bring / carry / fetch).
  - **EN**: to translate
    **MT**: Wassal xi ħaġa jew xi ħadd lejn post ieħor (bring / carry / fetch).
  - **EN**: to render [with b’ ‘something’]
    **MT**: Wassal xi ħaġa jew xi ħadd lejn post ieħor (bring / carry / fetch).
  - **EN**: to publish, to make public (in newspapers)
    **MT**: Wassal xi ħaġa jew xi ħadd lejn post ieħor (bring / carry / fetch).
  - **EN**: to expose to people's gossip
    **MT**: Wassal xi ħaġa jew xi ħadd lejn post ieħor (bring / carry / fetch).
  - **EN**: to imagine
    **MT**: Wassal xi ħaġa jew xi ħadd lejn post ieħor (bring / carry / fetch).
  - **EN**: to esteem
    **MT**: Wassal xi ħaġa jew xi ħadd lejn post ieħor (bring / carry / fetch).
  - **EN**: to behave
    **MT**: Wassal xi ħaġa jew xi ħadd lejn post ieħor (bring / carry / fetch).
  - **EN**: to grow
    **MT**: Wassal xi ħaġa jew xi ħadd lejn post ieħor (bring / carry / fetch).
  - **EN**: to cause sexual orgasm
    **MT**: Wassal xi ħaġa jew xi ħadd lejn post ieħor (bring / carry / fetch).
- **Alternative Forms**: ġieb
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Ġab il-paket mill-uffiċċju tal-posta kmieni filgħodu.
    EN: He brought the parcel from the post office early in the morning.

    **MT**: In-nom verbal ta' ġarr, il-proċess ta' trasport u ttrasportament (transport / carriage).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Il-ġarr tal-merkanzija kien responsabbiltà tas-sid.
    EN: The transport of the goods was the owner's responsibility.

    **MT**: Ġab, wassal xi ħaġa minn post għal ieħor (bring / carry).
  - **EN**: to procure, to cause
    **MT**: Ġab, wassal xi ħaġa minn post għal ieħor (bring / carry).
  - **EN**: to fetch (a price), to sell for
    **MT**: Ġab, wassal xi ħaġa minn post għal ieħor (bring / carry).
  - **EN**: to form, to give shape (to something)
    **MT**: Ġab, wassal xi ħaġa minn post għal ieħor (bring / carry).
  - **EN**: to work out, to form, to calculate
    **MT**: Ġab, wassal xi ħaġa minn post għal ieħor (bring / carry).
  - **EN**: to guess, to succeed
    **MT**: Ġab, wassal xi ħaġa minn post għal ieħor (bring / carry).
  - **EN**: to turn, to change (something)
    **MT**: Ġab, wassal xi ħaġa minn post għal ieħor (bring / carry).
  - **EN**: to translate
    **MT**: Ġab, wassal xi ħaġa minn post għal ieħor (bring / carry).
  - **EN**: to render [with b’ ‘something’]
    **MT**: Ġab, wassal xi ħaġa minn post għal ieħor (bring / carry).
  - **EN**: to publish, to make public (in newspapers)
    **MT**: Ġab, wassal xi ħaġa minn post għal ieħor (bring / carry).
  - **EN**: to expose to people's gossip
    **MT**: Ġab, wassal xi ħaġa minn post għal ieħor (bring / carry).
  - **EN**: to imagine
    **MT**: Ġab, wassal xi ħaġa minn post għal ieħor (bring / carry).
  - **EN**: to esteem
    **MT**: Ġab, wassal xi ħaġa minn post għal ieħor (bring / carry).
  - **EN**: to behave
    **MT**: Ġab, wassal xi ħaġa minn post għal ieħor (bring / carry).
  - **EN**: to grow
    **MT**: Ġab, wassal xi ħaġa minn post għal ieħor (bring / carry).
  - **EN**: to cause sexual orgasm
    **MT**: Ġab, wassal xi ħaġa minn post għal ieħor (bring / carry).
- **Alternative Forms**: ġab
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Ġieb il-pakett tal-bosta diretti d-dar.
    EN: He brought the mail parcel directly home.

- **Root**: ġ-b-s
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: mediopassive of ġebbes
    **MT**: Il-forma medjopassiva ta' ġebbes, ġie applikat il-ġibs fuqha.
  - **EN**: inflection of ġebbes: second-person singular imperfect third-person feminine singular imperfect
    **MT**: Il-forma medjopassiva ta' ġebbes, ġie applikat il-ġibs fuqha.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Il-ħajt ġġebbes u sar lixxi għall-pittura.
    EN: The wall was plastered and became smooth for painting.

## ġġela (v-ġġela)

    EN: The consonant in this word geminates in Maltese speech.

    EN: You cannot generalise one person's experience to others.

- **POS**: noun
